## Runner feelings (looked at the real shots; low-effort player, bot quiet)
- year 1 · turn 0-2 · rank 3 → 2 → 1 · CONFUSED · rank slides in the first two turns with no choice of mine; same unexplained opening as lazy-1n/lazy-2 · m-rank-1-7.png, m-rank-24-53.png
- year 7 · «Vào lãnh cung» (rank 1 → 0) · FRUSTRATED/ODD · the card says «Ta bị Hoàng hậu xử tội (tội tư thông)» but Danh is 99 and the bot never took a romance action; the second quiet life (after lazy-2) punished for «tư thông» with no visible cause. Art is striking (broken lattice window, autumn leaves, two Phế phi silhouettes) · m-rank-938-122.png
- years 8-22 · BORED · 3519 buổi at ranks 0-1, 2031 taps; ~15 years in Lãnh cung with 280 promotion roads offered and 93 taken, none moved me out; nothing new to decide
- year 22 · «Tuần lục» book-cover overlay · ODD · the cover page is drawn over the live header: the date line «Tháng chạp, năm thứ hai mươi hai, hạ tuần» overprints the stat strip and the status chip row; the page hint «Vuốt trang hoặc chạm mép phải để lật» sits on top of the bottom tab row so «Hành sự / Hậu cung / Giao tình» are half covered · now.png
- year 23 · ending «Lãnh cung — Tuổi xuân qua đi» · FRUSTRATED · life ends at 40 as Phế phi with 1514 Bạc unspent and no cause line; closure feels like a timer, not a story
